A grown-up-sized long-sleeved V-neck printed bodysuit tee in bamboo-blend jersey, a one-piece T-shirt top joined to a boy-leg bodysuit bottom with hemmed leg openings, covered edge to edge in a glossy all-over digital print, designed to look like the outfit of a harvest king. The print covers every part of the garment edge to edge, from the neckline to the hem of the bottom. The top shows a straw cape. The shirt's sleeves are tailored to the outfit's cut, wide or flared where the outfit's sleeves flare, ending at the same slant or curve, with the print filling the whole sleeve shape. An attached hood at the neck is printed as a crown of wheat. Turn it round and the back carries the rear of the outfit, with nothing left plain. The outfit's cloth covers the whole piece in one continuous print, collar, shoulders, sleeves, body and leg openings all in the outfit's own colours and textures. The print is cut exactly to the garment, neck, shoulders, armholes, sleeves and hem are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. A one-piece printed bodysuit with a T-shirt top and a smooth, hemmed, leotard-style bottom. All textures, transparencies and sheens are printed patterns on solid cotton. The print is the garment itself, flat and unfolded over the garment, in its own cloth and colours. The suit is the whole harvest king outfit, not just a top, with a straw cape, a crown of wheat (as the hood), a sickle sewn or attached to the suit. It is shown in full, from the neckline down to the ankle cuffs. Novelty embellishments, holographic appliqué patches and fluffy sherpa patches at the elbows and knees. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

It is a complete costume of grim reaper in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a black hooded robe, a hood (as the hood), a scythe sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once.
